## 0 — METHOD (READ FIRST)

**`[F]` Extraction is mechanical and reproducible.** Every row below carries a file and a line number in one of the fourteen artifacts. Nothing in this register is authored: each `DISCOVERY NAME` is the artifact's own wording, truncated. Where truncation loses meaning, `SOURCE LOCATION` is the authority.

**`[F]` Canonicalization rule.** A discovery is one canonical entry when it is one *statement* in one *declaring* artifact. Two rules decide identity:

1. **Phase-scoped identifiers** — `GAP-R3-01`, `UNK-R4-02`, `A-R2-01`, `DIV-P-01`, `RES-01`, `FB-1`, `RU-01`, `SC-01`, `AMB-01` and the like embed their declaring phase, so the same string in a later artifact is the **same** discovery, carried. The earliest artifact in phase order owns it; every later occurrence is recorded in `DUPLICATES`.
2. **Artifact-local identifiers** — `A-01`, `UNK-01`, `E1`, `K-1`, `G-11`, `R-01`, `B-01`, `C-01`, `D-01` do **not** embed a phase. The same string in a different artifact is a **different** discovery. These are recorded as namespace clashes in §4 and keyed `Axx::ID` internally. This is `GAP-R-01` (*no global identifier-namespace registry*) reproduced from the outside.

**`[F]` Admission decisions are type-derived, not judged.** The mapping from `DISCOVERY TYPE` to `ADMISSION DECISION` is fixed and stated in §1. The only per-row judgements are the corrections and supersessions, and each of those is a statement one of the fourteen artifacts makes about another — never a statement this register makes on its own account.

**`[F]` `ADMITTED` here means *admitted to the register*.** It is not admission to `UNIVERSAL-CONSTITUTIONAL-META-REQUIREMENTS-FOUNDATION.md`, which does not exist at HEAD. Candidacy for that instrument is analysed separately in `WP-001A-CONSTITUTIONAL-INTEGRATION-ANALYSIS.md` and is recommendation only.

## 9 — WHAT THIS REGISTER DOES NOT DO

`[F]` It admits nothing into any constitutional instrument. `[F]` It resolves no gap, discharges no assumption, decides no undecidable, and closes no unknown. `[F]` It allocates no ownership: the `OWNER` column names the artifact that declared the discovery, which is not the same as a constitutional owner, and no artifact in the set holds authority to allocate one (`AUTHORITY = NONE` in all fourteen). `[F]` Every disposition is valid for the state of the fourteen artifacts as extracted, and for no later state.
